# Changelog

## [v0.0.83] - 2026-10-05

### Added

- criticalSection/executePlan: warnings from earlier successful steps are lost when a later step fails ([#6909](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6909))
- test(element-targeting): shared hierarchy arbitrary + ElementParser shape-tolerance properties ([#6472](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6472)) (android, ios, testing)
- feat(ios): implement bounded Files picker fixtures through putAppFile user_files ([#5807](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/5807)) (ios, testing)
- fix: handle iOS 16+ "Allow Paste" alert on non-English locales ([#1881](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/1881)) (ios)

### Changed

- Dead Code Detection: Threshold Exceeded ([#9649](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9649)) (automated, dead-code)
- Resolve the unreachable CoreDevice downgrade-guard seam in CoreDeviceCapabilityProbe (wire or remove) — deferred from #8944 ([#8992](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/8992)) (ios, bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)
- plan: PlanSchemaValidator re-probes 13 candidate paths and reparses the schema on every executePlan call ([#6532](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6532)) (performance)
- Optional bindFileBackedDbHarness() hooks binder (deferred from #3081 item 3) ([#3101](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/3101)) (testing)

### Fixed

- tapOn (android): tapping a disabled element reports success with no indication that the element is disabled ([#9766](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9766)) (android)
- homeScreen (android): called from the Recents overview it goes home but reports "Already on the home screen" ([#9757](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9757)) (android)
- tapOn (android): the semantic long press under TalkBack still sends a bare resource-id action, so it can act on a different row with the same id ([#9753](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9753)) (android)
- displayConfig (android): a successful non-reset call loses its message, and the night-mode baseline is never cleared when the device changes ([#9743](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9743)) (android)
- talkback(android): an id-addressed accessibility action can still hit an off-screen duplicate, because the host cannot see nodes the extractor drops ([#9742](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9742)) (android)
- accessibility(android): a persistently failing TalkBack probe costs an extra adb call on every observe and gesture, with no backoff ([#9725](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9725)) (android)
- navigation resources: with two device sessions the unscoped graph and history resources return the launcher with an empty graph ([#9723](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9723)) (android)
- displayConfig (android): reset reports "Font scale remained 1 after requesting default" although the reset worked, and turns a dark device light ([#9722](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9722)) (android)
- identifyInteractions (android): Compose buttons are classified as input fields and get a tapOn focus suggestion ([#9721](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9721)) (android)
- executePlan, appLifecycle, crashApp, getAppPermissions, highlight, recordSteps, exportPlan, explore: a failed operation is returned without isError ([#9720](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9720)) (android)
- sendKeys (android): type with no focused editable field reports success and types nothing ([#9719](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9719)) (android)
- daemon: a silent request with a budget above 15 minutes is cut by the idle-socket timeout before its own deadline ([#9718](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9718)) (daemon)
- observe (android): returns the previous app's hierarchy for up to 0.85 s after an app switch, flagged stale but not re-read ([#9706](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9706)) (android)
- tapOn (android): a tap sent after launchApp uses the mid-transition bounds from the unsettled cached observation, misses, and reports success ([#9705](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9705)) (android)
- tapOn (android): an element under the soft keyboard that extends below the keyboard view is tapped inside the IME's navigation bar and reported as success ([#9704](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9704)) (android)
- Windows flake: oxlintConfigScoping.integration.test.ts times out spawning "oxlint --print-config" (20 s hook timeout) ([#9702](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9702)) (ci)
- navigation: an observe hierarchy update without a package name still attaches another app's screen to the previous app's SDK node ([#9699](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9699)) (android)
- observe(android): hierarchy conversion drops enabled="false", so a disabled control reads as enabled and its state change never appears in a diff ([#9696](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9696)) (android)
- observe waitFor.absent: an unavailable or error hierarchy satisfies the wait on the first poll ([#9695](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9695)) (android, ios)
- observe(android): skeleton duplicate index ignores window order on linked-window captures, so index replays to the element in the other window ([#9694](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9694)) (android)
- daemon client: a call that expires while still queued in the daemon is reported as a plain client timeout — DaemonClient arms the same deadline as the daemon, cancels first, and discards the daemon's "never started" answer ([#9691](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9691)) (daemon)
- tapAt(android): coordinate tap ignores TalkBack, so it only moves accessibility focus yet reports a delivered tap ([#9690](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9690)) (android)
- tapOn(ios): a VoiceOver activation that times out after dispatch falls back to a second coordinate tap instead of reporting an indeterminate outcome ([#9689](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9689)) (ios)
- talkback: opt-in screen-reader navigation never reports focusTrapDetected, because executeTap rethrows the ActionableError the executor throws ([#9688](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9688)) (android)
- executePlan: a failed optional step leaves no trace in the response — success:true with executedSteps < totalSteps and no skipped-step list ([#9687](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9687)) (daemon)
- executePlan: cleanupAppId cleans only the primary device of a multi-device plan — other labelled devices return to the pool with the app running and its data intact ([#9686](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9686)) (daemon)
- executePlan (ios): a plan longer than 300s returns no video — the recording auto-stops at 300s and the plan-end stop then fails with "No active recording found" ([#9685](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9685)) (ios, video)
- executePlan (android): segmented plan-video warnings (truncated, gap, omitted segment) never reach the plan result or its segment manifest ([#9684](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9684)) (android, video)
- plan: a waitFor timeout fails the step only for a top-level observe — openLink waitFor and observe inside criticalSection are reported as passed ([#9683](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9683)) (daemon)
- accessibility(android): a failed ADB read is cached for 60 s as TalkBack off, and accessibility talkback:false then reports it already disabled ([#9682](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9682)) (android)
- talkback: tapOn, long press and swipeOn scroll act on the first node with a shared resource-id, not the selected one, and report success ([#9681](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9681)) (android)
- Windows flake: xcodegen drift check "ctrl-proxy scope passes when only the PBXProject target order changed" times out at 5000 ms ([#9674](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9674)) (ci)
- observe (android): while the system clipboard chip is showing, the observation contains only the chip and none of the app's content ([#9673](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9673)) (android)
- swipeOn lookFor (android): each scroll step flings a long list further than one screen, so rows are skipped and a nearby target is reported not found ([#9672](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9672)) (android)
- explore: a login screen at the root of the back stack makes the run press Back, leave the app and relaunch it in a loop until the timeout ([#9671](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9671)) (android)
- test(video): videoRecordingManager test lets an in-flight auto-stop rebuild real dependencies after teardown and hit the real-database guard ([#9669](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9669))
- ci: turbo lint inputs omit scripts/utils-import-direction-baseline.txt, so a baseline-only edit can replay a cached lint success ([#9662](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9662)) (ci)
- ci: the timing budget's isolated recheck runs without AUTOMOBILE_TEST_MODE, so its median is measured under different conditions from the unit lane it overrides ([#9661](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9661)) (ci)
- ci: the unit timing budget's 600 s recheck budget equals the PR job's 10-minute timeout, so its fail-closed report is unreachable and a slow recheck ends as a silent job timeout ([#9660](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9660)) (ci)
- ci: Fast Validation's hand-picked --only list omits registered checks, so the test as-any ratchet never runs in CI ([#9659](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9659)) (ci)
- ci: lint runs oxlint --fix, so an auto-fixable violation (such as a brace-less if) passes CI and the committed code stays violating ([#9658](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9658)) (ci)
- ci: detect-changes marks a PR docs-only from the first 30 changed files, so a PR with more than 30 docs files and a code file skips Node and native CI ([#9657](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9657)) (ci)
- videoRecording(android): a zero-byte device file after screenrecord exited is retained forever — stop can never succeed and the device stays blocked ([#9654](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9654)) (android, video)
- videoRecording(ios): a stop that fails after simctl has exited keeps the recorder owner forever — the simulator cannot record again and stop is retried every 5 s ([#9653](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9653)) (ios, video)
- videoRecording(android): a replacement segment that fails to start is only logged — the session records nothing more and stop reports success ([#9652](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9652)) (android, video)
- videoRecording(android): a segmented session whose rotation stop failed stays tracked forever — every stop throws and bare by-device stop is blocked ([#9651](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9651)) (android, video)
- plan video(android): a step that runs past the segment's 180 s auto-stop makes rotation and finalize fail, so the whole plan video is dropped ([#9650](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9650)) (android, video)
- sendKeys/clearText(ios): text requests use a fixed 5 s transport timeout regardless of text length, and a timed-out typeText is reported as a plain failure while the runner keeps typing ([#9646](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9646)) (ios)
- launchApp(ios): coldBoot/launchArguments/clearAppData on a bundle that is not installed returns the raw simctl error instead of "App is not installed" ([#9645](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9645)) (ios)
- explore: maxInteractions is documented as defaulting to 50 but the run uses 200 ([#9634](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9634))
- terminateApp: the result message says "Terminated app X" when the app was not installed or not running and nothing was terminated ([#9633](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9633)) (android, ios)
- setAppPermissions, executePlan cleanup (android): with a running work profile a personal-only app still resolves to the work profile — pm grant and pm clear target the wrong user ([#9632](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9632)) (android)
- nav graph: a hierarchy fingerprint from another app is correlated to the previous app's node when it arrives within 1 s of an SDK navigation event ([#9631](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9631)) (android, ios)
- pool: criteria-based multi-device allocation does not roll back devices already assigned when a later assignment attempt throws ([#9628](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9628)) (daemon)
- deleteDevice: a failed recheck while retiring a stopped target's pool entry keeps its shutdown reservation for the daemon's lifetime ([#9627](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9627)) (daemon)
- clipboard, keyboard, recentApps: a failed operation is returned in a non-error envelope (isError unset) ([#9625](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9625))
- clipboard(android): a paste, copy or clear that CtrlProxy refuses falls back to the unimplemented cmd clipboard path and reports success ([#9624](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9624)) (android)
- tapAt(android): a tap on a locked device returns a clean success with no deviceLock warning, unlike tapOn and the other gesture tools ([#9622](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9622)) (android)
- display actions: a failed post-action read on the selected panel reports a delivered tap, swipe or drag as a plain failure — one bad settle poll discards a good observation and invites a second gesture ([#9621](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9621)) (android)
- observe: the post-action settle gate drops the screenshot an action captured — with action screenshots enabled, tapOn and other navigation actions return no screenshotPath or screenshot URI ([#9619](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9619)) (android, ios)
- tapOn(android): the post-tap wait takes 2.1–2.5 s on a screen that is already still — it waits 1 s for a hierarchy push that never comes, then evaluates its 1 s quiet period only once per second ([#9618](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9618)) (android)
- ctrlproxy(android): storage-change fetch and destroy make blocking cross-process ContentResolver.call on the service main thread ([#9617](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9617)) (android)
- sdk(android): a previous-session ANR is marked reported after an unacknowledged sendBroadcast, so it is lost for good when CtrlProxy is not running ([#9616](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9616)) (android)
- ios-sdk: AutoMobileSDK reads eventBuffer and sessionTracker outside its lock in notifyNavigationEvent, recordWebViewEvent and currentSessionId ([#9615](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9615)) (ios)
- doctor: a failed simulator listing prints "[SKIP] Booted Simulators: 0" and hides the reason in the console report ([#9580](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9580)) (ios)
- terminateApp(ios): the terminated app's cached hierarchy can still be served, because the window and observe caches are not invalidated ([#9557](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9557)) (ios)
- sdk(ios): after shutdown with a later crash handler installed, re-initialize can leave AutoMobile's exception handler unreachable ([#9556](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9556)) (ios)
- sdk(android): the database inspector keeps serving a deleted-and-recreated database from its cached handle ([#9555](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9555)) (android)
- sdk(android): the SharedPreferences inspector reports a stale previous value after Editor.clear() below API 30 ([#9554](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9554)) (android)
- sdk(ios): AutoMobileSDK.shutdown() unconditionally restores the pre-SDK uncaught-exception handler, removing any crash reporter installed after AutoMobile ([#9531](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9531)) (ios)
- sdk(android): executeSQL runs only the first statement of a multi-statement query and reports success (iOS rejects it with multiple_statements_not_supported) ([#9529](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9529)) (android)
- record(android): a failed or exited getevent touch track is only logged — the recording keeps running and stop returns a tap-less plan as success ([#9528](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9528)) (android)
- setUIState: the default "down" field search swipes the finger down (toward content above), wasting 3 swipes before it can reach fields below the fold ([#9526](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9526))
- explore validate mode: stops at the first screen with no pending outgoing edge claiming "All edges in navigation graph have been traversed", and presses Back after deciding to stop ([#9525](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9525))
- junit-runner(android): executePlan error envelopes without a success field (session lost, device lost, daemon shutting down) are reported as a passing plan ([#9524](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9524)) (android)
- doctor: ADB Version always reports protocol version 1.0.41 instead of the platform-tools version, and passes with 'unknown' on unrecognised output ([#9511](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9511)) (android)
- doctor: Booted Simulators passes when simctl list failed, and Simulator Runtimes reports none installed when the output was unparsable ([#9510](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9510)) (ios)
- automobile:video/latest returns the most recently read recording instead of the most recently recorded one ([#9509](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9509))
- resources/subscribe returns Method not found through the stdio proxy, so resources/updated notifications never reach external clients ([#9508](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9508))
- videoRecording stop deletes the recording it just finished when it is larger than maxArchiveSizeMb, and still returns success with its filePath ([#9507](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9507)) (android, ios)
- Android: a fractional millisecond duration makes tap, swipe, drag and pinch requests fail to decode ([#9495](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9495)) (android)
- swipeOn lookFor (android): a failed or indeterminate scroll swipe is ignored, more swipes are dispatched, and the call reports "reached end of container … not found" instead of the swipe error ([#9490](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9490)) (android)
- wakeAndUnlock(android): the PIN is typed and Enter pressed without re-checking the keyguard after dismiss-keyguard, so on an already-dismissed lock the credential goes into the foreground app and an unverified PIN is remembered ([#9489](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9489)) (android)
- display(android): the automatic post-action screenshot captures the default panel after an action on an explicit or pinned display ([#9478](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9478)) (android)
- tests: unit suites that use the real Window read the persistent window cache from disk, so results depend on what another suite or the daemon wrote ([#9474](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9474))
- ios: fractional long-press/drag durations pass the tool schema but fail the runner's Int decode, so the gesture is rejected as a malformed request ([#9471](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9471)) (ios)
- ios: a runner hierarchy-extraction error is decoded as an empty success, so observe reports request_timed_out and loses the real error ([#9470](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9470)) (ios)
- ios-runner: hierarchy walk silently drops everything below raw depth 30 (even with disableAllFiltering), so deep screens observe as complete but miss elements ([#9469](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9469)) (ios)
- ios-runner: foreground detection only accepts com./io./org./net./me./dev. bundle ids, so other apps are observed as SpringBoard and the host drops fallbackToSpringboard ([#9468](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9468)) (ios)
- putAppFile (Android user_files and app_containers): a batch that fails part-way leaves earlier files written and the error does not say which ([#9467](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9467)) (android)
- installApp (Android): a failed post-install package listing rejects the call although the APK was installed ([#9466](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9466)) (android)
- installApp (Android): downgrade recovery uninstalls the app for every user but reinstalls it for one, and a failed reinstall does not say the old app is gone ([#9464](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9464)) (android)
- navigation: a recorded tool call is never withdrawn on failure or consumed on match, so edges are labelled with the wrong tool call and navigateTo replays it ([#9463](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9463))
- pressButton back/recent, recentApps and clipboard paste (Android): a CtrlProxy request that times out is re-sent through ADB, so the key is pressed twice and the call reports success ([#9462](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9462)) (android)
- daemon: a failed heartbeat or reclaim write leaves the session marked as heartbeat-received and owned, so the watchdog skips its first-heartbeat and owner-timeout expiry ([#9458](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9458))
- deleteDevice: a teardown failure that escapes the service is reported as operation_cancelled ([#9456](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9456))
- sdk(android): acknowledged event delivery is off in a normal install because the host app cannot see the CtrlProxy package ([#9451](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9451))
- video: a throughput limit below 1 Kbps disables the recording bitrate cap instead of applying it ([#9450](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9450))
- executePlan: a parallel plan reports success with zero steps run when startStep is past the end ([#9448](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9448))
- daemon: resources/read ignores the request timeout and client cancellation — its options are passed in an argument position the MCP SDK does not read ([#9444](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9444)) (daemon)
- Android: the daemon drops hint-text from CtrlProxy nodes, so tapOn/sendKeys/setUIState by a filled field's hint still fail ([#9439](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9439))
- ctrlproxy(android): select_all reports failure for an empty field showing its hint and for text that is already fully selected ([#9434](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9434)) (android)
- ctrlproxy(android): request_hierarchy gets no reply when extraction returns null, and request_hierarchy_if_stale error frames are dropped by owner routing — the host waits out its full timeout ([#9430](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9430)) (android)
- ctrlproxy(android): a streamed gesture keeps its pointer held down forever when the owning WebSocket client disconnects before gesture_end runs ([#9429](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9429)) (android)
- executePlan: per-step durationMs is the epoch timestamp outside debug mode, and is stored in test-execution step records ([#9427](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9427))
- terminateApp(android): a failed installed-package listing is reported as success with wasInstalled:false and no force-stop is sent ([#9426](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9426)) (android)
- navigateTo ignores cancellation: the handler drops the AbortSignal and the path replay keeps tapping after the request is cancelled ([#9424](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9424))
- navigateTo: after an intermediate screen is not reached, the remaining recorded taps are still replayed on whatever screen is showing ([#9423](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9423))
- observe(android): a failed node conversion leaves null entries in the CtrlProxy traversal array ([#9399](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9399))
- observe(android): getUiStabilitySnapshot's failure result omits updatedPrevTotalFrames (type error only; the method is unused in production) ([#9398](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9398))
- explore: non-string child attributes flow into the string fields text, class and content-desc ([#9397](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9397))
- observe: a record-shaped TimingData is spread as an iterable when building observe timings, which can throw ([#9396](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9396))
- daemon(android): setActiveDevice rebind waits on the old device's keep-awake restore with no deadline while holding the pool assignment mutex ([#9391](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9391)) (android, daemon)
- daemon: setActiveDevice rebind leaves the old device autolocked to the session that left it, so its next owner is refused as "locked to another session" ([#9390](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9390)) (daemon)
- daemon(android): System UI ANR recovery replaces a device without cancelling work bound to the old one ([#9389](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9389)) (android, daemon)
- observe(android): the file-pull screenshot fallback leaves /sdcard/screenshot_<id>.png behind when the request is cancelled — its rm inherits the aborted request signal ([#9388](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9388)) (android)
- tapOn/swipeOn under TalkBack: a click or scroll action that was sent but not acknowledged falls back to a gesture, so the element is activated or the list scrolled twice and the call reports success ([#9386](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9386)) (android)
- keyboard(android): detect and open trust a content-desc heuristic with no IME window — open reports "Keyboard already open" and does nothing on any screen labelled "Enter…" or "Delete" ([#9384](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9384)) (android)
- tapAt doubleTap: the second tap reuses the pre-tap frame context, so a first tap that changes the screen leaves one tap delivered and the call reported as failed ([#9383](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9383)) (android, ios)
- daemon(android): stopping idle emulators for an ADB-reset cohort is recorded as a watched-process-exit loss and relaunches them mid-detachment ([#9382](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9382)) (android, daemon)
- sdk(android): batch-id follow-ups — a flush that throws part-way replays without an id, and the dedup window is device-wide and lost on a CtrlProxy restart ([#9375](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9375)) (android)
- sdk(android): a late acknowledgement or a crash before delete makes a replayed event batch arrive twice — batches carry no id and CtrlProxy does not dedupe ([#9363](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9363)) (android)
- install: six more '… | grep -q' checks under pipefail can misreport a match as missing ([#9344](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9344))
- daemon: the in-window recovery error tells the caller both to acquire a new device and to wait — give it structured fields ([#9342](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9342))
- test(bats): install-dev-tools test 3 fails intermittently on CI — the installer reports success after Homebrew exits 42 ([#9326](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9326)) (testing)
- observe(android): a disconnected fold panel still gets the old error, and a pinned-panel error lists the unavailable panel as available ([#9322](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9322)) (android)
- record(android): a scroll along an axis the getevent stream never reported is dropped instead of recorded as swipeOn ([#9319](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9319)) (android)
- navigation: graph edges store __mcpRequestTimeoutMs, __mcpRequestDeadlineMs and sessionUuid, and navigateTo replays them with a stale deadline ([#9312](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9312)) (android)
- record(android): the first touch of a recording that repeats the slot's pre-recording X or Y is exported as tapAt with 0 on that axis ([#9311](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9311)) (android)
- observe(ios): row s2- ids still change with the SDK merge — normalizeIosHierarchy assigns ids without excludeSdkInjectedNodes, so the #9280 fix never runs for observe ([#9310](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9310)) (ios)
- daemon(android): after an emulator is lost, the next call on its session waits 120 s and returns a bare MCP timeout instead of the device-loss error ([#9288](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9288)) (android)
- iOS runner: request_hierarchy blocks ~60 s after an app with an unfocused text field is backgrounded; homeScreen fails and observe is unavailable ([#9285](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9285)) (ios)
- swipeOn(android): unnamed auto-target rejects a vertical list that is wider than tall (landscape, foldable inner display) and swipes from outside it ([#9284](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9284)) (android)
- daemon: the disconnect monitor cancels the session that is running killDevice before it checks the shutdown reservation ([#9277](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9277))
- observe(ios): a backward device clock step makes the older-push guard drop genuinely newer hierarchy pushes until the next solicited fetch ([#9274](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9274)) (ios)
- playground(android): ReorderableGrid maps drags with a hard-coded 96 dp cell and a stale index; wrong drop cell on a foldable inner panel ([#9262](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9262)) (android)
- killDevice(ios): the settle check always ends with a 1 ms simctl read, adds about 10 s, and can return device_lost for a kill that succeeded ([#9261](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9261)) (ios)
- observe(ios): a row's s2- id changes with the SDK merge (injected _UISystemBackgroundView child), so an id from observe can fail in tapOn with Element not found ([#9260](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9260)) (ios)
- setUIState(android): a later field is refused as covered by the soft keyboard that the call's own earlier field raised; no dismiss or scroll, the call fails ([#9258](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9258)) (android)
- InputText: the IME-action branch has no production caller, so #9116's "failed after the text was entered" result is unreachable; input/typeText and sendKeys surface the raw runner error ([#9188](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9188)) (dead-code)
- playground(android): "Go back" on a deep-linked video (including the new "Video not found" state) crashes — NavDisplay backstack cannot be empty ([#9140](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9140)) (android)
- iOS runner: request_hierarchy blocked about 94 s in the keyboard-focus frame query after wakeAndUnlock; every tool fails with "runner is busy" ([#9082](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9082)) (ios)
- bench(settled-screenshot): stop restarting the resident daemon, record failure reasons, flag all-failed series ([#8757](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/8757))
- Device identity quarantine seams: readiness cached clients, sessionless cancellation, stale refresh ordering, raw-serial video discovery ([#6929](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6929))
- navigation: extend the "unobservable ≠ dismissed" fix to the other lenient dismissal paths (isModalDismissed + back-button fallback) (deferred from #6682) ([#6748](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6748)) (bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)
- daemon: per-socket sequential request chaining lets a slow request (appearance's per-device pool loop, webrtc-stream's client timeout) block a queued stop/later request ([#6680](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6680)) (performance)
- plan: PlanNormalizer logs full raw and normalized step JSON at INFO, including user-supplied params ([#6667](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6667)) (maintenance)
- video(android): segment rotation stop() is awaited inline in the plan step loop with no timeout or abort, and is skipped entirely for multi-device plans ([#6660](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6660)) (android, video)
- plan: a device-loss rethrow from one parallel track never aborts sibling tracks, which keep driving devices after the plan returns ([#6658](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6658)) (enhancement)
- observe(android): getDeepLinks reports success for a missing package because it never checks the 'Unable to find package' marker ([#6644](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6644)) (android)
- plan: three independent MCP-envelope unwrappers fail open toward "success" on parse failure ([#6534](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6534)) (maintenance)
- plan: parallel device tracks cancel the base device's screenshot jobs, never their own ([#6533](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6533))
- accessibility(android): TalkBack consent-dialog dismissal is never confirmed, and toggle() reports applied from the settings value alone ([#6499](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6499)) (android, needs-human, needs-device-verify)
- plan: criticalSection and barrier rewrap DeviceLostError, hiding device loss from PlanExecutor ([#6468](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6468))
- plan: plan-execution active flag is a process-wide boolean, so the first of two overlapping plans re-arms the monitor ([#6396](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6396))

### Other

- ci: the unit timing validator does not say in the job log whether the isolated re-run also exceeded the budget ([#9767](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9767)) (ci)
- ci(shell): wire the pipefail-grep-q guard into the fast-validation gate and shrink its baseline (follow-up from #9533) ([#9708](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9708)) (bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)
- devices: setActiveDevice and getAndroid say the device is not found when Android discovery failed, without the discovery reason ([#9371](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9371))
- desktop-core: getDaemonStatus failure is swallowed to a blank default instead of surfacing an error state ([#9359](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9359)) (bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)
- playground(android): ReorderableGrid hard-codes its 8 dp spacing in three places, and the computed height has no test ([#9343](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9343)) (android)
- sdk(android): the ANR 'processed once per launch' test asserts nothing, and the timestamp-only reportNewAnrs overload is test-only ([#9282](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9282)) (android)
- ios-runner: the debouncer orders hierarchies by wall-clock updatedAt, and each command capture takes a second main-actor hop to record ([#9278](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9278)) (ios)
- bug(tapOn): unbounded longPress duration outlives the request — the press stays held on the device and later taps report success but do nothing ([#9275](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9275)) (bot-filed, routine:exploratory)
- tapOn ensureChecked picks the non-toggle row when a row and its switch share a label, instead of preferring the toggle candidate ([#9271](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9271)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- tools/list advertises debug-only tools (setUIState, identifyInteractions) that the daemon rejects, the error does not name --debug, and setToolEnabled reports enabled:true ([#9270](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9270)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- bug(swipeOn): boomerang apexPause/returnSpeed have no upper bound — raw 'input swipe … Infinity' failure, half-done gesture, and a reported duration that never elapsed ([#9263](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9263)) (bot-filed, routine:exploratory)
- ios(ctrlproxy): reusing the snapshot focus frame makes the first focused text input win and silently drops focused:true from other focused nodes ([#9248](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9248))
- sharedStorage: a failed rollback chunk repeats the command-embedding error message once per path, producing a multi-hundred-KB error ([#9247](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9247))
- observe(ios): resolveIosObserveRotation throws a raw TypeError when no screen size is available ([#9246](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9246))
- getDeepLinks: the tool wrapper turns a cancellation from DeepLinkManager into an error result instead of propagating it ([#9243](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9243))
- rotate(android): Idle.getRotationStatus still parses the old grepped mRotation dump, so rotating to landscape with a live mirror can time out ([#9235](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9235))
- ios: an out-of-range notification authorizationStatus is still reported as denied (allowed:false) instead of unknown ([#9234](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9234))
- sdk(android): broadcastHandledExceptionEvent ignores its timestamp parameter and stamps the broadcast time ([#9233](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9233))
- swipeOn(android): the new no-change warning fires on every successful boomerang swipe, where an unchanged screen is expected ([#9216](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9216))
- test: promiseRejectionOwnership test passes three constructor arguments to FakeObserveScreen, which has no constructor ([#9215](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9215))
- test(db): the migration self-contained guard rejects bare Node built-in imports such as "fs" ([#9214](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9214))
- input: a key dispatched before an abort surfaces as a bare cancellation, so a retry of enter/back can double-fire ([#9211](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9211))
- ios-sdk: network mock header matching picks an arbitrary header when two request headers differ only by case ([#9210](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9210))
- video-server: RotationMonitor logs one stderr line per 500 ms forever when its reader throws on every poll ([#9209](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9209))
- daemon: a failed removal on an intentionally killed device persists the incident as exhausted although no recovery was attempted ([#9206](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9206))
- daemon: the release-persistence-failure device-stuck-busy pattern remains in daemon.ts ([#9204](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9204))
- ime: the IME quarantine is never cleared when the device behind the serial is released or torn down ([#9203](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9203))
- rotate(android): readLiveRotation reads dumpsys window without the request's remaining time budget ([#9200](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9200))
- observe(android): a failed first display read is cached and stored as a single unknown display, and observe with an explicit display returns a device-access-error fallback ([#9199](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9199))
- observe(android): a screenshot capture aborted before dispatch still issues a pointless rm -f ([#9198](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9198))
- proxy: resources/read logs a cancellation as an error and wraps the abort in ActionableError ([#9197](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9197))
- tap(android): use the shared LONG_PRESS_MIN_MS constant instead of a bare 500 in coordinateTapDispatch ([#9196](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9196))
- sdk(android): an ANR that shares a timestamp with an earlier one is dropped for good, and a mid-read stream failure discards the trace ([#9194](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9194))
- hitTest and setPosture advertise raw/project observation options that do nothing ([#9148](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9148))
- CLI --daemon release-session logs "Fatal error in main(): write after end" and exits non-zero after succeeding ([#9068](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9068))
- uninstall.sh stops daemons with an unscoped pkill, and uninstall-desktop-app.bats runs it unstubbed: a BATS sweep kills the developer's live daemon ([#9056](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9056))
- CtrlProxy iOS WebSocket close reply drops the client's status code and reason ([#9023](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9023))

- sendKeys/tapOn focus (android): #8997 still fails on device — focused field is counted twice (hierarchy + windows[] copies) ([#9017](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/9017))

- swipeOn(ios): direction "down" starts inside the navigation bar (y≈80pt) on a scrolled list — no scroll, plain swipe reports success, lookFor reports "end of container" ([#8973](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/8973))
- doctor: CLI never reaches the daemon (doctor not registered daemon-side since #4601); silent direct fallback reports a live, port-reallocated iOS runner as not running ([#8974](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/8974))

## [v0.0.82] - 2026-10-03

### Added

- feat(observe): add a crop argument for element and rectangle screenshot crops ([#8759](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/8759)) (android, ios)
- pressButton: support volume_up/volume_down/power on the iOS simulator via private IOHID dispatch ([#8360](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/8360)) (ios)
- Remove the deprecated captureScreenshot MCP tool (PR 2 of #8042) ([#8148](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/8148)) (android, ios)
- Retire captureScreenshot: add a flag/env var for synchronous settled screenshots on every observation ([#8042](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/8042)) (android, ios)
- iOS Playground: tapAt target screen with visual-only large/small targets hidden from accessibility ([#8041](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/8041)) (ios, testing)
- Daemon: publish Android 'booting' lifecycle state and notify device resources at boot start (split from #4680) ([#7948](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7948)) (android, desktop, daemon)
- systemTray: replace substring resource-id classifiers with exact aliases ([#7674](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7674)) (android)
- observe: synthetic elementId resolves as a node key without becoming a native ID ([#7642](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7642)) (android, ios)
- observe: retain class and focus clues needed by selector resolution ([#7641](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7641)) (android, ios)
- chore(lint): forbid raw selector field reads outside toSearchable ([#7637](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7637)) (devxp)
- refactor(element-resolution): migrate tap, drag, and highlight ([#7636](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7636))
- refactor(element-resolution): centralize hierarchy capture and freshness ([#7635](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7635))
- refactor(element-resolution): route focus, tray, pinch, and swipe through resolver ([#7634](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7634))
- refactor(element-resolution): route debugSearch and waitFor through resolver ([#7633](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7633))
- feat(element-resolution): add pure selector resolver ([#7632](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7632))

- test(element-resolution): observe-to-resolve fixture round-trip and differential ratchet ([#7630](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7630)) (testing)
- stream: source-attested liveness so the idle heartbeat fires only when the capture source proves it's alive (PR 2 follow-up from #7580 / #7549) ([#7594](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7594)) (video, webrtc, desktop, bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)
- Desktop Logs: pane is dominated by ViewHierarchyExtractor "[OPT] -> KEEP" debug lines by default ([#7530](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7530)) (android, desktop)

### Changed

- Emulator integration script never releases its session, so the job's retry always fails ([#8609](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/8609))

- killDevice: a transient discovery failure in the post-release recheck retains the shutdown reservation for the daemon's lifetime ([#8002](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/8002))

- recovery: tracked-but-exited emulator still listed at the same serial is reported "stopped" without a kill or disappearance check, burning the crash-loop budget ([#7979](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7979)) (android)

- tap: index and selectionStrategy:random shrink the text candidate set, so index: 0 is not the default pick ([#6437](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6437))

- action: changeExpected compares view hierarchies by object identity, so "No visual change" never fires ([#6435](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6435)) (android, testing)

- observe: computeRequestedFreshness ignores `verified`, reporting an unverified cached tree as isFresh ([#6432](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6432)) (testing)

- appearance: sync scheduler skips a same-serial replacement device and its in-flight tick outlives stop() ([#6428](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6428)) (android)
- observe: cached observe results are keyed by serial alone and survive a daemon restart on disk ([#6427](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6427)) (android)

### Security

- observation-stream control commands (subscribe_storage, request_observation, request_navigation_graph) are unauthenticated — not covered by #5260's subscribe-only scope ([#7950](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7950)) (enhancement, daemon)
- Stream auth skips device scope when deviceId is omitted: video/webrtc/test-recording attach to another session's sole device ([#7949](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7949)) (bug, daemon)
- openLink(android): credential-bearing URI still leaks into tool errors — #7402 closed but its fix PR #7407 was never merged ([#7438](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/7438)) (bug, android, bot-filed, routine:manual-test)
- Video-stream relay: re-authorize/revoke pane subscriptions when device ownership changes (unowned-branch admission is not reconsidered) ([#5049](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/5049)) (android, ios, webrtc)

## Unreleased

### Changed

- Breaking: XCTestRunner plan tests now use `try await executePlan()` in `async throws` test methods. Synchronous MCP client methods, the executor's `sessionIdProvider` initializer parameter, and public `AutoMobileSession` are removed; use async clients and explicit `sessionUuid` or `idGenerator`. Suite timing prefetch preserves its synchronous boundary and existing ordering behavior. (#6061) (ios)
- The iOS SDK sub-package manifests (`ios/auto-mobile-sdk`, `ios/highlight-core`) now declare the same iOS 17 / macOS 15 floor as the published root `Package.swift` and `XCTestRunner` (iOS 17 was introduced in #6773). This is a breaking change for apps or tools that depend on those sub-packages directly and target iOS 15/16 or macOS 13/14. (#5839) (ios)
- The public iOS SDK types `NetworkFaultTransport`, `NetworkFaultAction`, `NetworkFaultRuleDTO`, `NetworkMockRuleStore.FaultRequest`, and `NetworkMockRuleStore.FaultDecision` gained `Sendable` conformance; `DatabaseInspector`, `NetworkMockRuleStore`, `NetworkCaptureRecorder`, `URLSessionNetworkCaptureAdapter`, `WebSocketNetworkCaptureAdapter`, `NWConnectionNetworkCaptureAdapter`, and `AutoMobileWebViewPolicy` now conform to checked `Sendable` instead of `@unchecked Sendable`. These changes are source-compatible and additive. (#5839) (ios)
- The iOS SDK types `AutoMobileBiometrics`, `AutoMobileFailures`, `AutoMobileNetwork`, `AutoMobileNotifications`, `AutoMobileInteractionTracker`, `ViewBodyTracker`, `NavigationAdapterHub`, `DeepLinkNavigationAdapter`, `CustomNavigationAdapter`, `UIKitNavigationAdapter`, `SwiftUINavigationAdapter`, `BlockNavigationListener`, `DefaultAutoMobileAPI`, `DefaultAutoMobileCrashesAPI`, and `DefaultAutoMobileNetworkAPI` now conform to checked `Sendable` instead of `@unchecked Sendable`. These changes are source-compatible and additive. (#5839) (ios)

### Removed

- Remove the deprecated `captureScreenshot` MCP tool. Use `observe({ screenshot: "settled" })` and its `screenshotPath` for a fresh, validated screenshot.

## [v0.0.72] - 2026-09-14

### Added

- Timing gate: key duplicate-named testcases by source line when Bun emits it, not by per-report ordinal ([#6948](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6948))
- killDevice/deleteDevice: add a `force` escape hatch for emulators whose console cannot confirm the AVD name ([#6864](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6864))

### Changed

- cache: installed-app write coordinator retains generation state for every device identifier forever ([#6704](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6704)) (performance, database)
- db: repository getDb helpers disagree on migration gating and produce different startup failure shapes ([#6703](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6703)) (database)
- db: eventRetention and rowCapRetention duplicate the same amortized row-cap state machine ([#6702](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6702)) (database)
- db: BunSqliteDialect broadcasts every query completion to all waiters and can starve queries behind transaction bursts ([#6699](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6699)) (performance, database)
- db: cached-statement hit re-reads PRAGMA schema_version on every query ([#6649](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6649)) (performance, database)
- ctrlproxy(ios): legacy-app uninstall probe runs on every setup() call, before the fast-path short-circuit ([#6575](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6575)) (ios, performance)

### Fixed

- VM snapshot failed-capture reclaim follow-ups: non-VM same-name rows, re-resolve the AVD before deleting, busy marker until the adb child exits ([#6970](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6970))
- exactDeviceProvisioning: compare hw.gpu.enabled during exact AVD matching, not only hw.gpu.mode ([#6963](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6963))
- listApps: thread the tool AbortSignal through catalog enrichment and queryInstalledApps ([#6954](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6954))
- Whole-response spill: derive the inline residue from the tool output schema instead of a hand allowlist ([#6950](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6950))
- Android screenshot requests cancelled after dispatch stay live in RequestManager and still publish to the observation stream ([#6934](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6934))
- Observe max_children output-cap marker: skeleton-with-raw-hierarchy and baseline-cap diff edge cases ([#6933](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6933))
- Tool parameter error rendering: union-arm issue selection follow-ups ([#6931](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6931))
- VM snapshot archive reclaim: concurrency and validation follow-ups ([#6930](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6930))
- enabledTools membership: derived label-session grants and post-commit read failures ([#6928](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6928))
- systemTray notification dumpsys parsing: remaining ambiguity/multiline-extras edge cases ([#6927](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6927))
- ObserveDiff projection drops per-observation metadata (truncationReasons, accessibilityAuditSkipped, …) under --actions-diff-observe ([#6926](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6926))
- ios: booted-device listing must treat omitted availability as available (isAvailable !== false), not truthiness — drops Booted sims ([#6902](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6902)) (ios, bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)
- getAndroid: pre-boot avdName+serial validation must run under the lifecycle lease and honor bootTimeoutMs ([#6899](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6899)) (android, bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)
- daemon routing: restore distinguished current session on socket reconnect; allow explicit free-device IDs in --no-proxy mode ([#6895](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6895)) (bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)
- CI: XCTestRunner Simulator Tests lane fails on 250 ms simctl-list probe during iOS videoRecording integration test ([#6857](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6857))
- stream(performance): timestamp parsing mis-reads numeric strings as years and limit has no cap ([#6677](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6677)) (maintenance, database)
- daemon: telemetry/failures/performance push sockets accept an invalid deviceSessionUuid and still ack success ([#6676](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6676)) (maintenance)
- daemon: all-device request_observation shares one 20s timeout across a sequential per-device loop ([#6669](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6669)) (testing)
- cache: CachingContentHashProvider.resolveContentHash is not single-flight, duplicating APK/IPA hashing under concurrent calls ([#6654](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6654)) (performance)
- cache: TTLCache.cleanup() is documented as automatic but never invoked, and size-capped eviction is O(n) per insert ([#6653](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6653)) (performance, maintenance)
- db: installed-apps cache freshness is device-wide MAX() but writes are single-row ([#6639](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6639)) (android, database)
- xctestrunner: StreamableHTTP client parses the daemon's SSE response body as JSON, so HTTP-transport calls fail ([#6633](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6633)) (ios)
- xctestrunner: device-pool preflight (ensureDevicePoolReady) is defined but never invoked ([#6627](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6627)) (ios, maintenance)
- mcp: swipeOn, dragAndDrop, pinchOn and inputText schemas silently drop unknown arguments (installApp{userId} too) ([#6613](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6613)) (maintenance)
- observe: exact/partial text bucketing ignores caseSensitive:false, so case-differing exact matches lose to unrelated exact matches ([#6607](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6607))
- observe(android): CtrlProxy screenshot capture ignores its abort signal for up to 10s, unlike the iOS path ([#6605](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6605)) (android)
- observe(android): processNodeChildren silently caps every node's children at 64 with no truncation marker ([#6601](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6601)) (android)
- observe(android|ios): observation/latest and observation/latest/screenshot resources can pair a hierarchy and screenshot from different devices ([#6600](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6600)) (android, ios)
- observe(android): screenshot cache lookup filters out .jpg, the default CtrlProxy capture format ([#6599](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6599)) (android)
- ctrlproxy(ios): physical-device team-ID detection always probes a repo-relative Xcode project path that installed builds don't have ([#6585](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6585)) (ios, performance)
- simctl: getScreenSize's LCD-section parser leaks uiScale/width/height across sections in `simctl io enumerate` output ([#6584](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6584)) (ios)
- observe(ios): BulletinBoard notification-authorization reader regex-parses NSKeyedArchiver plist and hard-codes the default device set ([#6583](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6583)) (ios, needs-device-verify)
- simctl(ios): SimulatorTccSqliteClient ignores CORESIMULATOR_DEVICE_SET_PATH and opens TCC.db read-write while tccd holds it ([#6582](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6582)) (ios, needs-device-verify)
- ctrlproxy(ios): manager instances and their port reservations are never evicted per device ([#6580](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6580)) (ios)
- ctrlproxy(ios): forceStopForShutdown tree-kills a captured PID with no ownership re-verification ([#6579](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6579)) (ios)
- simctl: shutdownSimulatorCoordinated's boot-lease wait is unbounded on CI-recovery and handle-cleanup shutdown paths ([#6577](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6577)) (ios)
- simctl(ios): device listing has no single-flight coalescing or last-good retention, unlike DevicectlDeviceLister ([#6576](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6576)) (ios, performance)
- simctl(ios): pushNotification reports success as failure when simctl writes advisory text to stderr ([#6517](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6517)) (ios, needs-device-verify)
- accessibility(ios): VoiceOverToggle reports a disable as applied when the confirmation probe never succeeded ([#6496](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6496)) (ios)
- snapshot(android): VM snapshots are recorded as 0 bytes and never deleted from the AVD ([#6490](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6490)) (android, performance, needs-human, needs-device-verify)
- snapshot(android): VM restore reports success after a fixed 2s sleep and invalidates no per-serial state ([#6489](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6489)) (android, needs-device-verify)
- db: crashes, anrs, tool_calls, and device_sessions grow without bound; the one pruning method is unreachable and its tool_calls filter never matches ([#6464](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6464)) (maintenance, database)
- ctrlproxy(ios): xctestrun runner-environment injection only understands the FormatVersion 1 layout ([#6418](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6418)) (ios, testing)
- ctrlproxy(ios): concurrent device setups share one unsynchronized builder that rm -rf's the derived-data root ([#6417](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6417)) (ios)
- ctrlproxy(ios): setup() answers "CtrlProxy was already running" from a 1-hour positive availability cache the iOS readiness path never reopens ([#6416](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6416)) (ios)
- ctrlproxy(ios): runner readiness gate accepts any /health responder — isRunning/waitForHealthEndpoint skip the device-identity check ([#6415](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6415)) (ios, needs-device-verify)
- deviceUtils: the iOS boot target falls back to a device name that the UDID-only verifier can never confirm ([#6414](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6414)) (ios)
- simctl: the two boot-completion paths disagree on isAvailable, so one resolves the boot the other rejects ([#6412](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6412)) (ios)
- simctl: boot retry treats an unreadable device state as a failed boot and re-boots into an in-flight shutdown ([#6411](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6411)) (ios, needs-device-verify)
- ctrlproxy(ios): resident xcodebuild runner inherits the per-request abort signal and is SIGTERMed on cancel ([#6410](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6410)) (ios)
- emulator: a mid-boot AVD is invisible to isAvdRunning, so a tmpdir pid file is the only duplicate-launch guard ([#6407](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6407)) (android, needs-human, needs-device-verify)

## [v0.0.71] - 2026-09-10

### Fixed

- logging: closeLogStream relies on an unverified WriteStream error/close ordering and can hang if close never arrives ([#6700](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6700)) (maintenance)
- tap(ios): refreshViewHierarchy drops the caller's remaining budget when fetching the iOS hierarchy ([#6615](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6615)) (ios)
- simctl(ios): privacy grants for multiple permissions run concurrently against one device's TCC.db ([#6581](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6581)) (ios, needs-device-verify)

### Other

- cli: the built-in help advertises forms the schemas reject — 'tapOn --text "Submit"' fails, union params render as the word "union", and bare --help starts a server then stack-traces ([#6817](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6817)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- systemTray(tap): returns the pre-tap shade as observationDiff.reason='diff_emitted', so an inline Reply that worked reads as a no-op ([#6816](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6816)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- mcp errors: schema-validation messages name leaf keys but not the object holding them — systemTray 'find' rejects the exact title/body/appId it tells you to pass ([#6815](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6815)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- mcp: acquiring a device session removes 57 of 84 tools from tools/list, and nothing names the 57 — an LLM client cannot reach setToolEnabled for a tool it can no longer see ([#6814](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6814)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- observe(android): the soft keyboard is enumerated key-by-key, so 69% of the skeleton is Gboard on any focused text field ([#6795](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6795)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)

## [v0.0.70] - 2026-09-10

### Fixed

- ctrlproxy(ios): shutdown's 1.2s/250ms budget cannot cover terminateProcessTree's TERM+KILL ladder ([#6578](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6578)) (ios)
- session: teardown preempts only the current lifecycle owner, so a queued start resumes against the destroyed device ([#6549](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6549)) (android, ios)
- daemon: shutdown watchdog is armed only on the stdin path, so a hung SIGTERM shutdown never self-exits ([#6541](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6541)) (enhancement)
- setActiveDevice: a device whose session is mid-release reads as free, so the rebind runs concurrently with that session's device teardown ([#6398](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6398)) (enhancement)
- bug(session): direct device binding bypasses pending cleanup quarantine ([#6365](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6365))
- sendKeys: required `platform` breaks device-targeting (+ post-execution freshness bound) — untracked residuals from PR #6053 ([#6345](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6345)) (bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)

### Other

- mcpVersion: `npm_package_version` outranks the package manifest, so a 0.0.69 install announces itself as 0.0.68 and is refused by the 0.0.69 daemon ([#6803](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6803)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- mcp: two blocking errors name the problem but not the next call (disabled-tool gating, multi-platform ambiguity) ([#6797](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6797)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- observe(android): skeleton label takes `text` over `content-desc`, so all four quick-settings tiles read "On"/"Off" and Wi-Fi is unidentifiable ([#6794](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6794)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- #6069 foreign-session guard (#6687) does not cover autolock ownership: an autolock-pinned connection still routes a genuinely-issued FOREIGN session to a device it never acquired ([#6729](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6729)) (bot-filed, routine:adversary, needs-human)
- bug(daemon): recover package-launched clients with unknown version identity ([#6245](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6245))

## [v0.0.69] - 2026-09-10

### Changed

- db: migration locking relies on untested Kysely SqliteAdapter contracts ([#6701](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6701)) (testing, database)
- logging: checkAndRotateLog performs a stat syscall on every serialized log write instead of on an interval ([#6651](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6651)) (performance)
- snapshot: no property test pins that resolved snapshot paths stay inside the snapshots base directory ([#6493](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6493)) (testing)
- Dead Code Detection: Threshold Exceeded ([#6290](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6290)) (automated, dead-code)

### Fixed

- clearText(android): reports success when no editable node is focused, diverging from inputText/selectAllText ([#6733](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6733)) (android, bot-filed, routine:manual-test)
- getIosSimulatorCapabilities: human-readable device name (e.g. "iPhone 17 Pro Max") falsely reported as having no BiometricKit support ([#6731](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6731)) (ios, bot-filed, routine:manual-test)
- retry: aborting a RetryExecutor backoff leaves the losing timer alive until its full delay ([#6707](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6707)) (maintenance)
- performance: PerformanceTracker misparents timings when concurrent branches mutate its shared cursor ([#6706](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6706)) (maintenance)
- utils: BufferQueue.peek(0) crashes on an empty queue and discard is quadratic in chunk count ([#6705](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6705)) (performance, maintenance)
- db: retention amortization counter resets before the in-progress guard, dropping the gate under sustained write bursts ([#6657](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6657)) (database)
- db(navigation): setNodeModals/setEdgeModals/setScrollPosition delete-then-insert outside a transaction ([#6656](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6656)) (database)
- db: DatabaseHealthProbe.check() arms two independent same-duration timeouts with no enforced ordering ([#6655](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6655)) (database)
- db: provisionDevice operation store restarts provisioning for a 'running' row instead of reporting in-progress, and never prunes its table ([#6652](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6652)) (maintenance, database)
- snapshot: on-disk settings.json/metadata.json are never read back; legacy scan only sees flat manifest.json ([#6492](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6492)) (maintenance)
- snapshot: concurrent captures run overlapping archive-limit evictions and over-evict ([#6491](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6491)) (database)
- db: linkUIElementsToEdge has no onConflict/delete-before-insert and can violate the edge_ui_elements unique index ([#6463](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6463)) (database)
- simctl: remainingBootTimeoutMs throws a bare Error, so the same boot deadline surfaces in two different shapes ([#6413](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6413)) (ios, maintenance)
- bug(session): warm getAndroid acquisition overwrites another agent’s live autolock owner ([#6363](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6363))
- fix(record): fall back to inputText when Android capability lookup rejects ([#6361](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6361)) (android)
- fix(android): cancel pending DocumentsUI capability and node-action requests ([#6360](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6360)) (android)
- shared-storage tools "advertises defaulted fields as optional" flaps the 100ms unit budget ([#6313](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6313))
- DefaultIosVoiceOverDetector converts unknown/timed-out VoiceOver state to false, so tapOn/tapAny can report a focus-only coordinate touch as successful activation ([#6267](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6267))
- AI recovery resumes mid-barrier-generation and deadlocks barrier-containing plans ([#6234](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6234))
- PerformanceAuditor touch-latency reuses a stale inert point across taps (TOCTOU) ([#6228](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6228))
- createToolExecutionContext ignores tool deviceReadiness on persisted daemon-session path ([#6227](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6227))
- MemoryMetricsCollector: coarse (second-resolution) device clocks can drop boundary-second GC events (#6125 follow-up) ([#6212](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6212)) (android)
- rotate: confirmation-timing edges — settle-wait before post-restore read; preserve confirmed rotation if auto-rotate restore write fails (#6129 follow-up) ([#6211](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6211)) (android)
- WcagAudit touch-target fallback density (320) creates false positives on real mdpi devices with unreported density ([#6196](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6196)) (android, a11y)
- logPruner sweep can unlink daemon-launch-<pid>.log while the daemon still holds the inherited fd open ([#6194](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6194))
- device provisioning: exclude release bounds below the CtrlProxy APK minSdk (24) so an over-old AVD isn't created then fails APK install ([#6187](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6187))
- observe: incomplete-capture warning misdiagnoses non-null-root causes (zero-area/offscreen discard, extraction throw) as 'no root' + isAccessibilityTool advice ([#6184](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6184))
- deviceMatcher: full ordering of lettered Android release qualifiers (12L, QPR) between numeric versions ([#6182](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6182))
- flake: DetectIntentChooser test hits 20s timeout under full parallel bun test run ([#6174](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6174))
- Flaky: DetectIntentChooser 'should detect intent chooser when provided with view hierarchy' times out (5s isolated / 20s in turbo test) ([#6173](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6173))
- homeScreen and pressButton{home} report success but do not leave the foreground app on Android API 28 (raw adb KEYCODE_HOME works) ([#6147](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6147)) (android, bot-filed, routine:manual-test)
- secretParameters key-name parsing: full YAML-escape/fold/line-ending decoding (hand-rolled scanner best-effort) ([#6141](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6141))
- DefaultUIStateSetup.tapCloseButton returns true after the first candidate regardless of outcome — "Cancel"/"Dismiss"/"×"/"✕" are never tried ([#6123](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6123))

### Other

- bug(session): keep recovery route lease through final binding ([#6726](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6726))
- ios: expose typed bound-session loss across shared daemon restart ([#6724](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6724))
- Complete GestureClassifier property coverage, including zero-scale pinches ([#6717](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6717))
- Define cross-platform device resource maps ([#6692](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6692))

- Undeclared tool arguments are silently ignored despite additionalProperties:false — setPreference{fileName} writes the default prefs file and reports verified:true ([#6348](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6348)) (bot-filed, routine:manual-test)
- #6292 fallback only matches "inspection is disabled"; the SDK's "mutations are disabled by SDK policy" branch keeps the setPreference/removeKeyValue asymmetry ([#6347](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6347)) (bot-filed, routine:manual-test)

- bug(daemon): shared daemon exit invalidates every active bound device session with no recovery path ([#6336](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6336))

- observe: activeWindow packageName-backfill replaces the whole object, zeroing layoutSeqSum/activityName and masking screen-change detection (#6239 regression) ([#6320](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6320)) (bot-filed, routine:adversary, needs-human)
- navigation: tapCloseButton reports a false-positive modal dismissal when the re-observation yields no view hierarchy (#6279 gap) ([#6319](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6319)) (bot-filed, routine:adversary, needs-human)
- performance: TouchLatencyTracker reports never-tapped initial point as touchCoordinates when the final latency sample is obstructed (#6296 regression) ([#6318](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6318)) (bot-filed, routine:adversary, needs-human)
- SharedPreferences write/delete asymmetry: setPreference succeeds (verified:true) while removeKeyValue/clearKeyValueFile/setKeyValue report "SharedPreferences inspection is disabled" for the same app+file ([#6292](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6292)) (bot-filed, routine:manual-test)

- Home-press verification: deadline-bounding + AbortSignal propagation across every device read (PressButton/Window/AdbClient/Explore) ([#6289](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6289))
- internalTimeoutParamProvenance.test.ts exceeds the 100ms unit budget (McpTestFixture round-trip) — main-red on Node Unit Tests ([#6286](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6286))
- Post-tap hierarchy-only transient settling under device-clock skew (coherent design) ([#6284](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6284))
- executePlan does not propagate the enclosing live deadline into internal tool steps (setUIState partial-results) ([#6277](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6277))

- `--daemon restart` can leave the old daemon alive and start a second one on another port; the orphan keeps CtrlProxy forwarding ownership and bricks the device with a "runner did not become responsive" error ([#6260](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6260)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- session_ownership_lost and "Tool X is disabled for device session" state the problem but never name the call that fixes it (getAndroid / setToolEnabled) ([#6259](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6259)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- effect.screenChanged reports false when a tap opens a dialog (activeWindow basis never falls through to viewHierarchy), and diff-mode observations omit activeWindow/freshness ([#6258](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6258)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- Settings switch rows (Wi-Fi, Airplane mode) expose only `tap` with no `toggle`/`checked`, so a client can flip Wi-Fi but cannot read or verify its state ([#6257](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6257)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)
- Skeleton now drops every zero-affordance node, taking the screen's state readout with it: a client cannot read the timer value it just entered ([#6256](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6256)) (bot-filed, routine:dogfood)

- tapAny is broken on iOS for all three actions — calls tap/doubleTap/longPress, which do not exist on IOSCtrlProxyClient (errors are in the tolerated typecheck baseline) ([#6248](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6248)) (bot-filed, routine:manual-test)
