## [v0.0.68] - 2026-09-06

### Added

- setToolEnabled: persist tool-selection-profile provenance across daemon restarts/upgrades (#6148 follow-up) ([#6225](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6225))
- TouchLatencyTracker: flag animating apps and move the synthetic tap inside the app window ([#6167](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6167)) (android, performance)
- networkCondition schema/plan-schema alignment with the runtime classifier (edge inputs) ([#6090](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6090))
- Recovery/Retry no-ops on a wedged daemon (socket-open but ide/status times out) — make ensureReady protocol-health-aware / forced restart ([#6082](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6082)) (desktop)
- feat(desktop): recovery / auto-restart affordance on the workspace status dot (Start-daemon parity with the device picker) ([#6035](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6035)) (desktop, bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)
- feat(desktop): reconnect affordance for DeviceStreamView panes after relay loss (sibling of #4868) ([#4962](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/4962)) (desktop)
- Desktop workspace: make overlay panels modal to keyboard/a11y focus ([#4846](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/4846)) (a11y, desktop)
- Desktop workspace: reflect Storage-facet edits live (observation stream + optimistic update) ([#4709](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/4709)) (desktop)

### Changed

- chore(deps): remove stale dependabot.yml swift entry for nonexistent /ios/XCTestService ([#6062](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6062)) (bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)
- Hardening follow-ups from #4655 (proxy release-record pruning, per-kind discovery epoch, resolver coupling) ([#4689](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/4689))

### Fixed

- logger: transient WriteStream open failure permanently discards all logs; JSON diagnostic breaks NDJSON (from #6111) ([#6179](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6179))
- networkCondition TTL: preserve deadline on failed reset + mirror TTL maximum in plan schemas (from #6113) ([#6178](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6178))
- networkCondition TTL: expiry cleanup lacks per-condition generation guard, can return a shaped device unrestored (P1, from #6113) ([#6177](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6177))
- barrier lags criticalSection in Android ValidTools and PlanValidator (plan-validation parity) ([#6175](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6175))
- parseArgs: --port / --host / --initial-session-uuid swallow the next flag when given no value (proxy-mode sibling of #6136) ([#6168](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6168))
- setToolEnabled accepts a never-issued sessionUuid and reports success, while every other session tool rejects it (#6069 follow-up) ([#6148](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6148)) (bot-filed, routine:manual-test)
- Daemon client path: platform-aware + non-destructive connectOnce/waitForReady (Windows named-pipe e2e + downstream stale-socket unlink) ([#6140](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6140))
- PlanExecutor.captureFailureObservation cancels its timeout with global clearTimeout (not the injected Timer) and skips cancellation on the error path ([#6139](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6139))
- DefaultRetryExecutor leaks one abort listener per retry on the caller's AbortSignal (never removed when the sleep wins the race) ([#6138](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6138))
- IOSCtrlProxyProcessClient.isRunning treats `kill -0` EPERM ("Operation not permitted") as "not running"; the stderr guard is unreachable ([#6137](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6137)) (ios)
- parseDaemonArgs swallows the next flag when --host / --plan-execution-lock-scope / --tool-outputs-dir is given no value (`--host --debug` starts a non-debug daemon) ([#6136](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6136))
- daemon/heartbeat is queued behind in-flight tools/call on the same socket, so a bound session can be reaped (heartbeat-timeout) during a long unattributed acquisition like startDevice for a second device ([#6135](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6135))
- iOS hierarchy stream diff never flags a node that only moved/resized: `nodeSignature` stringifies the iOS `$.bounds` object to "[object Object]" ([#6134](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6134)) (ios, desktop)
- Paginated table-data resource template only matches when both `limit` and `offset` are present in that exact order; handler and description say each is optional ([#6133](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6133)) (database)
- startDevice min/maxOsVersion means release version to the device matcher but API level to the Android provisioner, so one value cannot satisfy both (provisioning fails or re-creates AVDs) ([#6132](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6132)) (android)
- FileDownloader Node-HTTP fallback hangs forever when the server closes the connection mid-body (no response error/close handler, truncated file left behind) ([#6131](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6131))
- NavigationRepository.getOrCreateUIElement never matches falsy identifiers (`""` text, `0` bounds) — inserts a duplicate `ui_elements` row on every tap ([#6130](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6130)) (database)
- rotate (Android) reads `user_rotation`, which is stale under auto-rotate, so a landscape device is reported "already in portrait" and never rotated; auto-rotate is left disabled ([#6129](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6129)) (android)
- swipeOn OverlayDetector counts the container's own clickable/focusable ancestors as overlays, forcing the "No unobstructed swipe area" fallback ([#6128](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6128))
- WCAG touch-target check compares physical pixels to a 44 dp threshold without density scaling (33 dp targets pass on xxhdpi, px reported as dp) ([#6127](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6127)) (android, a11y)
- explore resetToHome re-fires on every iteration while interactionCount sits on a multiple of resetInterval (including 0), then never relaunches the app ([#6126](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6126))
- Memory audit GC metrics are always 0 on ART: logcat filter (`-s dalvikvm:I art:I | grep "GC_"`) and regex only match Dalvik-era output ([#6125](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6125)) (android, performance)
- TouchLatencyTracker only counts a "response" when a jank counter increments, so a smooth app is reported as frozen and touchLatencyMs is always null ([#6124](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6124)) (android, performance)
- explore permission-dialog detection/dismissal still uses bare substring keywords ("access", "ok") — same defect class as #4190 ([#6122](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6122))
- explore passes both `text` and `elementId` to tapOn, so every element with text + resource-id fails "exactly one selector" validation and is never tapped ([#6121](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6121))
- logPruner: daemon's own-file cap claims peer `daemon-launch-<pid>.log` files, deleting the daemon's rotated backups while launch logs are never swept ([#6120](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6120))
- PortManager.allocate() rejects every host port on hosts without an IPv6 loopback (::1 bind failure treated as "port busy") ([#6119](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6119))
- MCP progress notifications never reach clients: tools/call handler fabricates progressToken instead of echoing `_meta.progressToken` ([#6118](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6118))
- barrier plan step drops its `device` label at schema.parse, so every track's barrier routes to the base session's device and fails as a duplicate arrival ([#6117](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6117))
- TalkBack swipeOn maps finger direction to the inverted ACTION_SCROLL (up → scroll_backward), so lookFor scrolls away from the target ([#6116](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6116)) (android, a11y)
- Plan schema false-rejects params-overridden inline setDeviceState fields (doNotDisturb + audit; networkCondition done in #6090) ([#6112](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6112)) (enhancement)
- secretParameters parser: multiline YAML flow-sequence declarations drop keys (fail-open redaction gap) ([#6097](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6097))
- doctor host-command timeout sends SIGTERM with no SIGKILL escalation — a wedged check can hang past DOCTOR_EXEC_TIMEOUT_MS ([#6018](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6018)) (ios, bot-filed, routine:tracker-hygiene)
- iOS video-recording integration test (#5984) can never assert: skipped by default, and fails on the videoRecording session tool gate when enabled ([#5987](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/5987)) (ios, testing, bot-filed, routine:manual-test)
- #5976 still returns the non-focused status-bar window on API 34, now with verified:true and no warning — observe yields status-bar-only rows and every tap fails ([#5981](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/5981)) (android, bot-filed, routine:manual-test)
- release: GitHub Release upload is the last step, so a late-job failure leaves an npm version with 404 asset URLs until manual re-dispatch ([#4687](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/4687)) (release engineering)

### Security

- security(android): authenticate CtrlProxy storage provider callers ([#6011](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/6011)) (android, bot-filed, needs-human)
- [epic][security] iOS video capture security hardening ([#4774](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/4774)) (ios, research)
- [security] Android video server hardening — threat model & tracking ([#4738](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/4738)) (android, video, research)

- testExecutionRepository.recordExecution: wrap multi-insert in a transaction (atomicity) ([#3403](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/3403)) (database)

- fix(db): guard runMigrations with a cross-process lock so two DB openers can't trip a spurious startup failure ([#2794](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2794)) (database)

- fix(db): reset failed migrationsPromise and surface a cached startup error synchronously so a floating rejection can't kill the daemon ([#2786](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2786)) (database)
- fix(db): destructive migration auto-recovery (default-on) can DROP every table on a populated database ([#2785](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2785)) (database)

### Fixed

- Windows CI: databaseLazyPath + dbWriteBarrierResetOnClose file-DB tests still flake (EBUSY/livelock) despite #2916/#2923 ([#2992](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2992))
- jimp image tests fail locally (bun/macos-arm64) while passing in CI ([#2971](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2971)) (javascript)
- test(nav): isolate NavigationGraphManager.test.ts singleton-backed blocks to fix cold-first-run flakiness ([#2969](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2969))
- fix(db): transactionalize promoteSuggestion + recordHierarchyNavigation graph writes (siblings of #2791) ([#2968](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2968)) (database)
- Flaky CI: windows-latest file-backed SQLite close/reopen tests (EBUSY/livelock) intermittently fail ([#2916](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2916)) (database)
- Flaky CI: DefaultProcessExecutor "exec captures stdout" times out (30s) on macos-latest; spawns real subprocess ([#2914](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2914))
- iOS runner: widen gesture request coordinate fields to Double (Int decode + roundCoordinates:false is a latent robustness gap) ([#2909](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2909)) (ios)
- fix(db): migration columns store literal "datetime('now')" instead of a timestamp (48 columns, 24 migrations) ([#2895](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2895)) (database)
- fix(db): route NavigationGraphManager shutdown write through the DB-write barrier without perturbing nav/hierarchy ordering (follow-up to #2792) ([#2885](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2885)) (maintenance, database)
- fix(db): wrap FailureAnalyticsRepository.recordFailure in a single transaction (close count-ahead-of-occurrence window) ([#2877](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2877)) (database)
- fix(cli): direct-mode DB guard transiently over-refuses during daemon startup (publish dbPath before DB open) ([#2871](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2871)) (database)
- fix(daemon): daemon process detection is POSIX-only (ps), breaking singleton enforcement + forcing direct-mode guard fail-open on Windows ([#2870](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2870))
- fix: 6 remaining TS2304 out-of-scope identifiers (2 latent runtime ReferenceErrors in Capture/RestoreSnapshot) ([#2866](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2866))
- fix(cli): refuse --no-proxy/--direct when a live daemon already owns auto-mobile.db ([#2795](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2795)) (database)
- fix(db): guard runMigrations with a cross-process lock so two DB openers can't trip a spurious startup failure ([#2794](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2794)) (database)
- fix(db): drain in-flight DB writes before closeDatabase() so shutdown cannot strand on Kysely ConnectionMutex ([#2792](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2792)) (maintenance, database)
- fix(db): make NavigationGraphManager.recordNavigationEvent graph write transactional ([#2791](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2791)) (database)
- fix(db): non-transactional get-or-create / read-modify-write races across coverage, navigation, prediction, and appearance repositories ([#2790](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2790)) (database)
- fix(db): add UNIQUE index on failure_groups.signature and make recordFailure an atomic upsert ([#2789](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2789)) (database)
- fix(db): reset failed migrationsPromise and surface a cached startup error synchronously so a floating rejection can't kill the daemon ([#2786](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2786)) (database)
- fix(db): destructive migration auto-recovery (default-on) can DROP every table on a populated database ([#2785](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2785)) (database)
- fix(daemon): make startup DB/migration failure fatal so the daemon doesn't run query-dead while reporting healthy ([#2784](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2784)) (database)
- fix(talkback): FocusNavigationExecutor.navigateToElement throws ReferenceError on zero-swipe path (accessibilityService out of scope) ([#2765](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2765)) (android, a11y)
- fix(ios): setDeviceState binary Do Not Disturb does not verify on iOS 18.6 simulator (notifyutil read-back unchanged) ([#2545](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2545)) (ios)

## [v0.0.40] - 2026-06-30

### Added

- Cross-platform tool to read/write app preferences (adb prop / SharedPreferences / iOS UserDefaults) ([#2714](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2714))
- install.sh should detect and help install the required Android SDK platform (compileSdk) ([#2680](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2680))

### Fixed

- doctor reports all-green while iOS observe is broken (no client↔runner round-trip check) ([#2693](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2693))
- iOS CtrlProxy forced-restart recovery should use PortManager to clear processes bound to the target port ([#2692](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2692))
- iOS CtrlProxy port management collides with adb; client and runner ports diverge ([#2691](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2691))

### Other

- Stamp git SHA into pre-release daemon version so the version gate distinguishes dev builds ([#2738](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2738))
- Verify build-mismatch daemon restart doesn't worsen the #2599 session-wedge for sibling MCP sessions ([#2737](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2737))
- doctor: surface daemon build identity (entryScript / buildId) to spot wrong-build skew ([#2736](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2736))
- doctor: iOS round-trip check can't detect runner/client port mismatch ([#2735](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2735))
- fix(ios): simulator runner ignores allocated port (binds 8765) when Android is connected → client/runner port mismatch, all iOS ops fail ([#2731](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2731))
- Daemon log file is unlinked by bunx temp-dir cleanup; on-disk logs end up empty ([#2724](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2724))
- openLink times out at 30s on slow deeplinks, then logs success=true after the timeout ([#2723](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2723))
- Emulator launches windowed by default; aborts with opaque "code: null" on headless hosts ([#2722](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2722))
- Headless daemon start fails 10s budget: open -a Simulator → OSLaunchdErrorDomain Code=125 in no-GUI launchd session ([#2721](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2721))
- Remove heapdump native addon dependency for Node 26 compatibility ([#2719](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2719))
- iOS CtrlProxy reconnect cooldown is very long (~137s) and observe gives no 'reconnecting' signal ([#2695](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2695))
- Demote/clarify WARN: 'Failed to read simulator app bundle for ...XCTestServiceApp' during iOS setup ([#2694](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2694))
- iOS CtrlProxy runner reports 320x480 screen (missing launch screen) instead of real device size ([#2683](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2683))
- iOS highlight (SDK path): verify/fix coordinate scaling so highlights land on the correct element ([#2682](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2682))
- iOS highlight: remove non-SDK runner-overlay fallback and report honestly that it requires the AutoMobile SDK ([#2681](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2681))
- doctor: add iOS CtrlProxy runner check so iOS gives the same certain feedback as Android ([#2678](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2678))

## [Unreleased]

### Fixed

- iOS `highlight`: remove the non-functional runner-overlay fallback and report honestly that highlighting requires the in-app AutoMobile SDK ([#2681](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2681)) (ios)

- Consolidate duplicated device-vs-session resolution in SocketServer.getRequestArgumentScopeKey ([#2664](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2664))
- Trim over-broad Random/Backoff utils and unify three UUID sources onto IdGenerator ([#2663](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2663)) (dead-code)
- iOS network mock-rule sync is copy-pasted from Android (require() in ESM, any-typed, debug-swallowed) ([#2662](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2662)) (ios)
- appFileService: replace positional-boolean params in executeAndroidAppFileCommand with options object ([#2660](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2660)) (android, devxp)
- Refactor oversized orchestrators: split TapOnElement.ts (1.6k lines) and toolRegistry.ts (909 lines) ([#2659](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2659))
- Establish & document an error-handling convention (throw vs return vs log-and-continue) ([#2657](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2657))
- Refactor: de-duplicate DevicePool assignment methods (LRU drift) + remove left-in [DEVICE-POOL-DEBUG] logging ([#2656](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2656))
- Type safety: replace `any` in navigation hierarchy logic with existing ViewHierarchyNode type ([#2655](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2655))
- Dead Code Detection: Threshold Exceeded ([#2361](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2361)) (automated, dead-code)

### Fixed

- AndroidSystemConfigurationAdapter.setTimeZone reports success without read-back verification ([#2661](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2661)) (android)
- fix(ios): postNotification requires appId on iOS but the schema marks it optional ([#2544](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2544)) (ios)
- fix(ios): SDK in-app hierarchy (:8766) merged into observes of unrelated foreground apps — ~2.5MB hierarchies ([#2540](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2540)) (ios)
- fix(intellij): #2464 device-disconnect deviceEvents wired only to uncalled LayoutInspectorDashboard (live view may still freeze) ([#2531](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2531)) (intellij plugin)

### Other

- doctor: make daemon health socket probes non-destructive ([#2658](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2658))
- putAppFile resolves a relative sourcePath against the daemon cwd (homedir), not the launch cwd — ENOENT/wrong-file under a detached daemon ([#2644](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2644))
- doctor: reports 'Daemon is not running' while it is serving, and prints device=emulator-5554 during an iOS-only run ([#2643](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2643))
- recentApps on iOS reports success but doesn't reach the App Switcher (lands on cover-sheet/home) ([#2642](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2642))
- launchApp returns a stale embedded observation (pre-transition app) — follow with a fresh observe ([#2641](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2641))
- clipboard get on iOS can return a stale in-memory 'shadow' value, masking a failed/changed real read ([#2640](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2640))
- pinchOn returns 'not supported on iOS yet' even though the iOS runner advertises request_pinch ([#2639](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2639))
- Daemon DisconnectMonitor churns on phantom/removed devices: stale 'Failed to stop recording' every ~5s + repeating miss counts (+ emulator resurrection) ([#2638](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2638))
- iOS SDK: network-mock enforcement compiles into RELEASE builds (mock short-circuit sits outside #if DEBUG) ([#2637](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2637))
- Generated schemas/tool-definitions.json is missing 10 production tools — generator skips 5 register*Tools categories ([#2636](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2636))
- Pre-commit hook crashes: planYaml.ts uses js-yaml APIs removed in v5 (blocks all commits) ([#2635](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2635))
- Clarify maxDurationSeconds behavior for iOS stream-copy remux ([#2627](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2627))
- Add iOS simulator videoRecording start-stop MP4 integration test ([#2628](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2628))
- Keep plan-executable tools available inside criticalSection ([#2614](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2614))
- highlight on iOS has no working path for non-SDK apps: runner-overlay fallback disabled by default (AUTOMOBILE_IOS_LIVE_HIGHLIGHTS) ([#2607](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2607))
- videoRecording stop fails on iOS simulator: mov→mp4 FFmpeg finalize produces no output and the error hides the cause ([#2606](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2606))
- getAppPermissions fails on iOS simulator: sqlite3 called with '.mode json' + SQL in one positional arg ('extra argument') ([#2603](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2603))
- Daemon restart desyncs connected MCP clients: all tool calls + resource reads return opaque 'Session not found' with no auto-recovery ([#2599](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2599))
- clipboard get on Android returns empty success when the read is denied by ClipboardService (background a11y), and cmd clipboard fallback doesn't exist ([#2596](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2596))
- changeLocalization silently fails on Android: reports success but the system locale never changes (invalid 'cmd locale set-locales', no-op fallback, no verification) ([#2595](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2595))
- sqlQuery: INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE ... RETURNING on Android commits the mutation but returns an error (RETURNING not recognized by isReadQuery) ([#2594](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2594))
- CtrlProxy APK refresh blocks every tool call on slow/no network (~30s each); local ops like sqlQuery hang offline ([#2590](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2590))
- tool_calls.duration_ms is never populated (NULL for all rows) — per-call timing not recorded ([#2588](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2588))
- iOS startDevice/listDeviceImages returns "Available images: none" despite a booted, available simulator ([#2572](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2572))
- MCP tool errors discard subprocess stderr / daemon-log context, surfacing only opaque messages ([#2562](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2562))
- Multi-daemon detection false-positive: a single daemon's own shell wrapper is counted as an "other" daemon ([#2561](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2561))
- Daemon inherits spawner cwd; a deleted cwd makes forked children fail ENOENT, misreported as "Android emulator not found" ([#2560](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2560))
- Concurrent instances crash on startup with SQLiteError: database is locked (no busy_timeout) ([#2559](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2559))
- openLink lacks an MCP request-timeout floor; slow deeplinks fail with -32001 ([#2558](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2558))
- junit-runner and manager.ts hardcode @latest, defeating consumer version pinning ([#2386](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2386))
- Detect Compose interop content hiding and signal OCR fallback ([#2374](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2374))
- launchApp rejects 'packageId' parameter — LLM commonly uses wrong field name ([#2217](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2217))

## [v0.0.38] - 2026-06-25

### Added

- feat(android): accessibilityFocus — set/clear TalkBack cursor focus ([#2503](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2503)) (android, a11y)
- feat(ios): biometricAuth — Touch ID / Face ID simulation via simctl Darwin notifications ([#2497](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2497)) (ios)
- feat(ios): getNotificationPolicy — read per-app notification authorization on simulators via BulletinBoard ([#2496](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2496)) (ios, research)
- feat(ios): postNotification — deliver simulated push to target bundle via simctl push ([#2495](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2495)) (ios)
- feat(ios): deviceSnapshot — capture and restore simulator settings (includeSettings) ([#2494](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2494)) (ios)
- feat(ios): setDeviceState — Do Not Disturb mode fidelity and physical-device support ([#2493](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2493)) (ios, research)
- feat(ios): getDeepLinks — URL scheme & universal link discovery via Info.plist ([#2486](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2486)) (ios)
- feat(ios): dragAndDrop — coordinate drag via XCUITest CtrlProxy runner ([#2481](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2481)) (ios)

### Fixed

- fix(ios): daemon runs the published-release CtrlProxy runner, not source — new runner commands fail "Unknown command type" against a stale binary ([#2539](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2539)) (ios)
- fix(ios): CtrlProxy WebSocket and AutoMobile SDK server collide on port 8766 — all iOS observe/gesture ops fail (Expected 101) ([#2538](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2538)) (ios)
- fix(daemon): signal/exception handlers registered inside main() after dynamic imports — startup window with no cleanup (regression from #2465) ([#2532](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2532)) (release engineering)
- fix(ios): observation stream double-pushes initial hierarchy frame on cold subscribe ([#2530](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2530)) (ios)

### Other

- Client can silently use a daemon of a mismatched version (@latest spawn + restart cooldown) ([#2452](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2452))
- Crash/force-kill leaves stale PID and socket files; no exit-time cleanup ([#2451](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2451))
- Device disconnects are not surfaced to observation-stream subscribers ([#2450](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2450))
- Screenshot backoff stops after initial burst; live view freezes on static screens ([#2449](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2449))
- Observation stream pushes no initial frame on subscribe; idle devices show blank ([#2448](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2448))
- Observation-stream request_observation command is a no-op; socket clients cannot force a frame ([#2447](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2447))
- Daemon stream socket paths are not discoverable, causing silent connect failures on env mismatch ([#2446](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2446))
- Device pool never prunes shut-down devices, wedging discovery until daemon restart ([#2445](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2445))
- Daemon readiness inferred from PID/socket-file existence, not an actual connection ([#2444](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2444))
- Device held ~30s when a client dies before its first heartbeat ([#2443](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2443))
- iOS device pool never auto-boots an installed-but-shutdown simulator to satisfy criteria ([#2442](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2442))
- Daemon version skew is only reconciled one direction; stale daemon can serve newer clients ([#2441](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2441))
- Add a fast `--version` flag; today it hangs by falling through to the stdio server ([#2440](https://github.com/kaeawc/auto-mobile/issues/2440))

## [0.0.6] - Unreleased

### Changed

- Placeholder: started fork; release notes not recorded.

## [0.0.5] - 2025-07-22

### Added

- Add ADB command auto retry

### Changed

- Improve view hierarchy search for elements with auto retry
- Refactor ConfigurationManager

### Fixed

- Test authoring fixes

## [0.0.4] - 2025-07-08

### Added

- Automate Accessibility Service install and enablement on first ADB command for a device
- Check APK SHA256 before installing
- Allow element search to restrict to specific containers in the view hierarchy

### Changed

- Increase accessibility node inspection for better view hierarchy data
- Filter unnecessary view hierarchy data to reduce observe payloads
- Parallelize and cache observation and idling steps
- Refactor BaseVisualChange
- Reduce tool calls to make it easier for agents to determine tool call selection

## [0.0.3] - 2025-07-08

### Added

- Automate Accessibility Service install and enablement
- Check APK SHA256 before installing
- Allow element search to restrict to specific containers in the view hierarchy

### Changed

- Increase accessibility node inspection for better view hierarchy data
- Filter unnecessary view hierarchy data to reduce observe payloads
- Parallelize and cache observation and idling steps
- Refactor BaseVisualChange
- Reduce tool calls to make it easier for agents to determine tool call selection
